# Skill Backlog

Use this file as the canonical list of skills to create, improve, or validate in this repository.

## Status Values

- `candidate`: possible skill idea; not yet committed.
- `planned`: accepted skill to create or update.
- `in-progress`: actively being implemented.
- `validating`: implementation exists and needs validation.
- `done`: validated and ready to use.
- `rejected`: intentionally not pursuing.

## Backlog

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

## Recent Validation Notes

- `vectorize-art-patterns`: Expanded on 2026-07-24 from the published eight-item comparison fixture to a 300-member collection with 15 organic, stain, ink, and Cubist-derived collage families sourced from six verified open artworks by Hilma af Klint, Juan Gris, Wassily Kandinsky, Alice Bailly, and Odilon Redon. Seeded crop, affine rotation, and smooth remap displacement create independent source compositions before tracing. The build enforces 300 unique composition hashes, 300 unique complete-geometry hashes, 1,300 globally unique path signatures, 300 unique seeds, 150 members per colorset, visible red/blue palette anchors, no `<use>`, and `tile=none`. The generated 602-file SVG/report inventory reproduces byte-for-byte with SHA-256 `996528be8230a456dde46d64a4f9c542a8e82e8a6276de63fc0e43e4ca825e00`; the manifest SHA-256 is `4926a5c21ac7e1886d3d2bb28fb65547edab39667af7f5f063ab85784d5a16d6`. The dynamic Pages fixture exposes family/mode/colorset/search filters, 24-card pagination, stable direct links, provenance, and lazy SVG loading. Local Playwright checks pass at 1,440 × 1,000 and 390 × 844 with zero overflow or console errors; 60 stratified images from every family render successfully. The all-item raster audit reports 55.11% median, 21.71% fifth-percentile, and 4.75% minimum contrasting coverage, with no pattern below 1%. Core tests cover both colorset anchors, same-seed determinism, different-seed composition change, and invalid variation bounds. PR `#20`, merge `d5073f434a6d2d476042c97689357b7e903ec22f`, and successful Pages workflow `30132325089` published the replacement at `https://gvillarroel.github.io/skills/examples/vectorize-art-patterns/`; production fetched and hash-verified all 300 SVGs and repeated the full browser audit with zero console errors. Isolated Spark remains externally blocked by provider quota, so the skill status stays `validating`. Durable record: `evaluations/vectorize-art-patterns-20260724.md`.

- Repository-wide: Migrated the canonical skill source from `.agents/skills/` to the tracked top-level `skills/` directory on 2026-07-22. `.agents/skills/` is now an ignored repository-local installation maintained by `scripts/sync-local-skills.py`; the synchronizer preserves additional local files and its post-sync check matched all 3,230 canonical source files. Updated repository validators, the isolated Pi harness, Pages publishing, project helpers, diagrams, evaluation commands, and in-skill operational paths to use `skills/`. Corrected D3 and Slidev fixture output paths for the one-level shallower source tree. Passed pattern-ID validation (1,236 IDs), repository validation, skill-independence tests, payload validation, all 11 Pi harness tests, Python and Node syntax checks, a full Pages build with 651 files, and Mermaid/PlantUML coverage. The default-sandbox Mermaid batch had one transient `stateDiagram` renderer failure; the trusted-fixture rerun with `--disable-browser-sandbox` approved all 96 renders with zero unresolved cases.

- `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Removed the AI concept acceptance fixture's final repository dependency on 2026-07-12. The fixture now bundles self-contained `scripts/render-videos.mjs` and `scripts/review-videos.mjs`; package scripts no longer invoke `projects/ai-concept-videos/scripts/`. Both runners resolve the fixture from their own location, default artifacts to an operating-system temporary directory outside the skill, accept explicit external paths or `AI_CONCEPT_VIDEO_ARTIFACTS_ROOT`, reject writes inside the skill bundle, and expose non-writing `--dry-run`/`--help` paths. Source checks passed runner syntax, `npm run validate`, `npm run render:check`, `npm run review:check`, explicit external-path dry-runs, and the read-only output guard. An isolated full-payload copy under `evaluations/runs/html-fixture-runner-20260713T023623Z/skills/` passed both runner dry-runs, fixture content validation, a Playwright render-state smoke, a 0.2-second one-frame quick render, and the local review runner with artifacts outside the copied skill. `uv run --script scripts/validate-skills.py` and `uv run --script scripts/test-skill-independence.py` passed after the promotion.

- Repository-wide independence: On 2026-07-12, added static self-containment gates to `scripts/validate-skills.py` for direct sibling-skill paths, `__file__` repository-root ascents, out-of-bundle symlinks, broken in-bundle Markdown links, and missing literal runtime resources. Added `scripts/test-skill-independence.py` with clean and negative fixtures, including fenced-code and non-skill `skills/hooks/MCP` false-positive regressions, and added the test to AGENTS and evaluation Gate 0. The audit covered all 21 skills and repaired direct fixture coupling in `animated-svg-to-gif` and `manim-svg-video`, standardized normal-use command paths across multiple skills for the isolated `skills/<name>` layout, generalized the HTML video prompt wrapper to safe workspace-relative roots beyond `projects/`, removed runtime recipes that required acceptance galleries or project-only render scripts, and corrected the D3 logo studio's texture-atlas link contract. `test-skill-independence.py` and the integrated repository validator pass.

- `d3-animated-svg`: On 2026-07-12, removed repository-root palette dependencies from the CS1/colorset2 gallery verifiers by bundling exact palette color sets under `assets/palettes/`; both Chromium verifiers passed 224/224 SVGs with zero paint findings and palette sizes 17 and 37. Promoted the 450,114-byte `d3-agent-loop-overlay` PNG from the acceptance fixture to `assets/reference/`, kept the mirror byte-identical, and added deterministic `scripts/build_agent_loop_overlay.py` after a first naturalistic isolated run exposed that manual image embedding was too error-prone. The builder emits standalone HTML with the PNG as a data URL, five canonical region IDs, SVG-native reveal motion, reduced-motion final state, replay, no network/repository/skill paths, and a compact SHA-256 contract report. Local self-contained checks and Chromium capture passed; visual review corrected the initial oversized viewBox from 980x618 to 560x400. Strict Spark contract run `20260713T025500Z-d3-agent-loop-contract-pi` passed the exact command/output, event/model, and unchanged-payload gates; isolated SHA-256 `4984df3b…` exactly matches the browser-tested local artifact. Naturalistic samples produced correct artifacts but failed strict trace gates because the sampled agent issued malformed ad hoc shell checks; classify those runs as `agent`, retain them, and repeat the naturalistic case before treating this independence revision as a new D3 release.

- `diagram-type-coverage` and `mermaid-colorset-styler`: On 2026-07-12, Pages runs `29193307188`, `29193946671`, `29194418933`, and `29195096628` exposed unstable long-lived Mermaid CLI 11.16.0 rendering on Ubuntu. The gate now uses chunk-first rendering: all 96 diagrams start in twelve independent eight-item processes with one render job, valid partial output is promoted after a nonzero exit or timeout, only unresolved diagrams are retried, and stubborn items are isolated as singletons under a 600-second overall budget. Run `29195717274` then proved that all 120 chunk attempts failed before rendering, and diagnostic run `29196111814` captured the exact Chromium error: the GitHub-hosted runner's AppArmor policy rejected every launch with `No usable sandbox`. The validator now accepts an explicit `--disable-browser-sandbox` mode that writes a temporary Puppeteer config with `--no-sandbox` and `--disable-setuid-sandbox`; the option remains off by default and the Pages workflow enables it only for trusted repository-owned fixtures through `validate-diagram-type-coverage.py --disable-mermaid-browser-sandbox`. Reports preserve stable 1–96 and per-colorset mappings, attempt/timeout/nonzero counts, exact unresolved declarations, browser-sandbox mode, and sampled stderr for the Pages wrapper. Deterministic tests cover chunk sizing, timeout-prefix promotion, singleton isolation, terminal failure, default sandbox preservation, and the exact trusted-CI Puppeteer config. Local live validation passed 96/96 with 12/12 first-attempt chunks and zero findings; an independent comparison confirmed eight-item batches are faster than four-item batches. `test_style_mermaid_directory.py`, the chunk-first tests, the combined seven-check gate, `validate-pattern-ids.py`, `validate-skills.py`, `check-repo-payload.py`, the Pages build/format gate, and targeted diff checks pass. Strict isolated runs `mermaid-colorset-styler-ci-serial-20260712-spark-1` and `mermaid-colorset-styler-chunk-first-20260712-spark-1` both observed `gpt-5.3-codex-spark` but the provider returned a usage-limit error before the first token or tool call, so the skill remains `validating`; rerun the unchanged prompt when quota is available.

- `awsome-videos`: Hardened finished-video visual orchestration on 2026-07-09. The skill now routes frozen URL-backed facts and fact-to-shot mappings, scene composition, semantic transitions, specialist assets, and exactly one renderer through evidence-bound contracts; it scaffolds `source-package.json`, `shot-contract.json`, `asset-manifest.json`, `composition-plan.json`, and `transition-plan.json` while keeping the generic HTML starter final-ineligible. Ready assets require in-root visual/media files, provenance/rights, exact SHA-256s, substantive producer checks, raster/SVG decoding, ffprobe-valid video, or structurally valid GLTF/GLB. Composition requires stable beat/scene/object IDs, rejected alternatives, anchors, text constraints, hierarchy, four motion phases, exact seams, and at least three materially distinct `objectBounds` layouts; the scaffold now provides eight. Renderer validation requires the visible media element to load the exact manifest resource, rejects hidden-wrapper/inline spoofing and broken media, validates geometry, hashes inputs, and detects markerless scaffold near-copies. Final review requires ordered, in-interval, globally unique-hash first/hold/emphasis/final and seam-straddling before/midpoint/after frames; all 53 fixture frames pass ffmpeg comparison against the exact MP4 at a `0.04` normalized-difference ceiling. Final package validation now live-reruns the browser renderer, compares fresh renderer frames to the MP4, recomputes readiness, validates current style/quality/motion/capture/audio hashes, and rejects fabricated or stale reports. The 19-check suite passed positive cases plus adversarial path escapes, text/broken binary assets, renamed identical layouts, reversed beats, repeated hashes, mistimed frames/seams, unrelated pixels, inline resource spoofs, broken media, markerless scaffolds, forged renderer/readiness/style reports, and manifest/CLI mismatches. A fresh scaffold passed structural visual, `scene-composition-director`, and `scene-transition-director` validation while remaining explicitly ineligible for final delivery; an 80-second render/report-binding smoke passed MP4 validation. Strict isolated Spark run `awsome-videos-visual-orchestration-20260709-spark-4-json` with `openai-codex/gpt-5.3-codex-spark` produced all 11 exact outputs, including eight spatial layouts, and passed structural, artifact, event, model, skill-integrity, and read-surface gates; summary: `evaluations/awsome-videos-visual-orchestration-20260709-spark-4-read-surface.json`. - `awsome-videos`: Promoted representative audio metrics, duration enforcement, concept-specific starter scaffolds, stricter brief labels, and package-source validation into the runtime corpus/production contracts on 2026-07-07. `assets/reference/corpus-summary.json` now includes `audioProfile` with 8 representative examples, `medianSilenceRatio=0.0035`, `maxSilenceRatio=0.041`, and `nearContinuousAudio=true`; `references/awesome-fireship-patterns.md` points music-bed and ducking guidance to that structured asset. `scripts/check_reference_completeness.py` now requires and reports the audio profile, while `scripts/scaffold_production_package.py` writes `--expect-duration` and `--duration-tolerance 1` plus production-notes/package-manifest required flags into generated package validation commands, fills hook/assets/audio/script sections, fills production notes, and replaces generic template beat rows with title/promise-specific visuals, mechanism terms, warnings, and callbacks. `scripts/check_video_brief.py` now fails blank or too-thin labeled fields such as `Cold-open line`, `First visual`, and `Audio cue`, and `scripts/check_production_package.py` now validates production notes and package manifests as first-class source contracts. - `awsome-videos`: Added first-class motion-report validation on 2026-07-07. `scripts/render_concept_video.py` now writes `--motion-report` JSON with strong and subtle frame-pair motion metrics, `scripts/check_video_artifact.py` consumes that report, `scripts/score_video_readiness.py` counts it as validation evidence, and `scripts/check_production_package.py` requires motion-report paths/flags in production notes and package validation commands. `assets/templates/production-notes-template.md`, `scripts/scaffold_production_package.py`, `SKILL.md`, and the production references now thread `motion-report.json` through render, video validation, readiness scoring, package validation, and generated manifests. `scripts/test_validators.py` added `motionReportDetection`, where moving synthetic frames pass and static frames fail, plus `scaffoldManifest.motionReportCommandOk`. Local `check_reference_completeness.py --json`, `test_validators.py --json`, quick validation, repo validation, payload check, `git diff --check`, and a 24-second render smoke under `evaluations/runs/awsome-videos-motion-smoke-20260707-local` passed; the smoke MP4 artifact validation consumed contact sheet, quality report, motion report, and capture manifest with `"ok": true`. - `awsome-videos`: Made motion-report thresholds short-video aware on 2026-07-07. `scripts/render_concept_video.py` now reports both configured and effective thresholds, lowering only the required sample/changing-pair counts when duration and sample rate make the default 20-sample gate impossible. `scripts/test_validators.py` now checks `motionReportDetection.shortMovingOk=true` with 9 samples while static frames still fail. - `awsome-videos`: Hardened `--require-audio` on 2026-07-07 so silent AAC streams no longer count as finished audio. `scripts/check_video_artifact.py` now runs ffmpeg `volumedetect` by default when `--require-audio` is set, reports `audio_level.meanVolumeDb` and `audio_level.maxVolumeDb`, and fails below permissive mean/max dB thresholds unless `--skip-audio-level-check` is explicitly used for silent previews. `scripts/check_production_package.py` passes the same audio-level controls through complete package validation, and `SKILL.md` plus `references/video-production-playbook.md` document the behavior. Local validation passed `test_validators.py --json` with `audioLevelParser.passed=true` and `silentPassesThreshold=false`; a sine-bed smoke MP4 passed with audio level data while an intentionally silent MP4 failed with mean/max volume at `-91.0 dB`. - `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Promoted lessons from `projects/ai-code-assistant-concepts` on 2026-06-27 after a fifteen-video produce/critique/improve loop. The loop found that contact sheets can create false visual defects when the tile layout has more cells than sampled frames, so `production-loop.md` now requires honest contact-sheet geometry and regeneration after final renders. It also found that shared lower-third symbols can collide with scene-specific chips in full-size frames even when mosaics look acceptable, so the review gate now requires full-size keyframe inspection of recurring rails, watermarks, packets, and labels. Project validation passed data checks, browser smoke across 75 screenshots, final 30 fps renders for 15 videos at 1920x1080 and 1260 frames each, and MP4 review with zero black/freeze failures.

- `d3-animated-svg`: Added a reusable dynamic-symmetry point audit on 2026-06-26 with `scripts/audit_dynamic_symmetry.py` and `references/composition-audit.md`. The script opens any SVG/HTML source in Chromium, extracts final browser-resolved points for the selected SVG or object, scores them against boundaries, center axes, thirds, fifths, golden/root divisions, diagonals, reciprocal armature lines, and guide intersections, and reports point-level nearest guides, farthest points, role alignment, balance, and thresholdable JSON output. Documented the workflow in `SKILL.md`, `references/gallery-patterns.md`, and the saturated task-overlap pattern reference. Validated the current `d3-pattern-asymmetric-task-overlap-saturated` object with 3,199 extracted points, 58.36% guide alignment, 8.66% node alignment, dynamic symmetry score 52.65, zero outside-frame points, center-role alignment 57.18%, terminal-role alignment 66.00%, and a balanced quadrant imbalance of 7.31%. - `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Added `references/scene-pattern-recipes.md` so approved AI concept scene patterns, shared model boxes, beat-module splits, render presets, and validation gates are stored in the skill instead of only in examples. Passed repo validation with `uv run --script scripts/validate-skills.py`.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Added `references/example-pattern-recipes.md` so promoted gallery examples document how to recreate document token quality blocks, extraction buckets, image partial covers, inline bar tables, sketchy overlays, model execution boxes, and token roulette sampling from stable `d3-pattern-*` IDs. Passed repo validation with `uv run --script scripts/validate-skills.py`.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 202 cards with `Agent Loop Partial Covers`, an image-backed SVG pattern that keeps a source diagram visible while animated translucent covers and sweep lines partially cover selected regions.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Polished `d3-pattern-document-token-extraction-buckets` by replacing brace-like curved extraction connectors with subtle straight guide lanes from the source page into each bucket.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 201 cards with `Document Extraction Buckets`, a single-page document pattern where colored word blocks are scanned in writing order, extracted into filler/correct/wrong buckets, and summarized with calculated unit totals, percentages, and block counts.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Updated the document token quality family so word blocks and row rules reveal in writing order: left to right within each line, then top to bottom through each document, with explicit `data-writing-index` and `data-writing-delay` attributes for browser-level audits.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 200 cards with `Document Token Quality Red`, a reusable variant of `d3-pattern-document-token-quality` where wrong spans use the brand red token instead of yellow while preserving exact accumulated word-length ratios, fixed inter-word gaps, and paragraph-like document layout.
- `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Added the approved Video 1 final-block lesson for cost handoff scenes: draw model-entering and model-exiting token flows below the enclosing model box so the box occludes tokens until they cross its boundary, and keep precise cost/pricing readouts in fixed secondary panels instead of attaching numbers to moving meter marks.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Refined `d3-pattern-document-token-quality` paragraph composition so word spacing is visually realistic. The layout now packs tokens into paragraph-like lines using a fixed inter-word gap, keeps spaces blank, draws subtle line rules under actual text rows, and avoids leaving tiny single-word residue lines while preserving exact length-weighted ratios.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 199 cards with `Document Token Quality`, a reusable document representation where light gray page rectangles contain dark gray rules and variable-length word blocks. The renderer accepts correct/filler/wrong proportions and validates them by accumulated word length, not word count; the included examples cover 20/70/10, 10/85/5, and 70/10/20 using green, gray, and yellow spans.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Cleaned up `d3-pattern-tangled-tree-levels` by removing the redundant low-opacity `bundle-stem` layer that duplicated the vertical portions of active cascade paths. The formerly cyan family route now uses the darker blue hover token, leaving only the real parent-child links visible during the level reveal.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Added approved inline-bar-table guidance for live/current model pricing comparisons: use only current directly comparable rows with verified values, omit no-data rows, avoid stale model families in the visible set, preserve per-column scaling, and use solid bars when comparing magnitude. Repo validation passed after the current video pass.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Simplified `d3-pattern-deep-learning-model-execution` to only render a square model frame with an internal animated MLP. Removed the previous input/output tokens, motion packets, arrows, model text, and border pulse so the pattern focuses on the network activation itself.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Removed translucent informational background fills from `d3-pattern-speculative-decoding-verify`. The verify sweep now uses a solid token color behind the label, token and badge rectangles render with opaque fills, and the reject token uses a darker outline for emphasis without relying on background transparency.
- `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Added the approved lesson that recurring visual-language marks must become shared components after approval. The Video 1 LLM model box was extracted into a shared helper and reused by the mechanism segment so adjacent beats preserve geometry, color, internal placement, and activation style instead of drifting through local redraws.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Updated `d3-pattern-inline-bar-table` so embedded bars are scaled against the largest visible value in their own numeric column. The pattern now uses per-column scales and exposes `data-metric-key`, `data-value`, `data-column-max`, and `data-bar-width` on each rendered bar for browser-level ratio audits.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Added stable per-example pattern IDs for every gallery card using `d3-pattern-${id}`. Cards now expose the ID as DOM `id`, `data-pattern-id`, SVG `data-pattern-id`, and a compact visible label; the gallery verifier now fails on missing, invalid, duplicated, or mismatched pattern IDs.
- `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Added render-cost tiers for standalone HTML+D3+Anime.js video production: `quick`, `draft`, `motion`, `fast`, and `final`. The `fast` tier keeps 30 fps, CRF 16, and device scale 2 while using cheaper encoding and skipping contact-sheet generation for faster near-final previews; representative 2-second render validation showed `fast` at 9.02s versus `final` at 12.47s, and the `fast` MP4 passed automated review.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Aligned the clean and sketchy Gemma comparison cards to a shared token role map so both use the same repository palette semantics: E4B blue, 31B purple, Q4 neutral gray, VM cost orange, light fills from token highlights, neutral grid/axis/panel colors, and no raw ad hoc colors. Passed targeted browser color audit plus gallery and repo validation.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 198 cards with `Gemma Compare`, a non-sketchy version of the Gemma model comparison scorecard using the same sourced benchmark, context, memory, hardware, and estimated GPU VM cost data as the sketchy version. `verify:gallery` now pins `--expected 198`; rerun gallery, served desktop/mobile, targeted replay, and repo validation after this expansion.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 197 cards with `Sketchy Gemma Compare`, a model comparison scorecard showing Gemma 4 E4B versus Gemma 4 31B across benchmarks, context, memory, hardware fit, and estimated GPU VM cost. Documented sketchiness as a reusable overlay for any D3 pattern, including scorecards, comparison cards, tables, and explanatory diagrams. `verify:gallery` now pins `--expected 197`; rerun gallery, served desktop/mobile, targeted replay, and repo validation after this expansion.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 196 cards with five sketchy variants of existing examples: beeswarm, streamgraph, treemap, connected scatter, and histogram. The variants follow Jo Wood's Plot sketchiness pattern conceptually by using seeded rough marks, double strokes, and hachure texture while preserving the repository palette and standalone SVG animation. `verify:gallery` now pins `--expected 196`; rerun gallery, served desktop/mobile, and repo validation after this expansion.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 191 cards with `Pocket Monster Stippling`, a weighted Voronoi stippling example inspired by Mike Bostock's Observable technique and driven by a deterministic analytic creature mask. `verify:gallery` now pins `--expected 191`; rerun gallery, served desktop/mobile, and repo validation after this expansion.
